These gains persist under the same three-iteration repair loop. These results provide bounded evidence that useful alternative latent continuations exist and that PRM-scored latent branch selection can improve executable outcomes in this setting without retraining the main generative model.

</details>


### [128] [PoCQ: Proof of Contribution Quality as a Lightweight Blockchain Consensus for Secure Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05642)
*Sudad Abed,Nasser Sabar,Abdun Mahmood,Mohammad Jabed Morshed Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出PoCQ，一种基于区块链的共识框架，通过声誉感知的验证和聚合机制来保障去中心化联邦学习的安全性，在多个基准数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化联邦学习虽然去除了中心化协调器的依赖，但仍然面临模型投毒攻击、不可靠的验证和高验证开销等问题，亟需一种既能检测恶意更新又能控制成本的鲁棒方案。

Method: PoCQ框架结合密码学承诺与轻量级范数验证来高效检测恶意客户端更新；采用基于声誉的共识机制，根据参与者历史贡献质量动态调整其影响力；区块链仅存储紧凑的审计元数据以保证可扩展性。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，PoCQ在高度非独立同分布（non-iid）的医学数据集上准确率提升34.1%，全局平均准确率提升11%，同时每轮验证时间平均降低21.27%。

Conclusion: PoCQ有效结合了鲁棒性和效率，为完全去中心化的联邦学习提供了一种可行的安全性增强方案。

Abstract: Decentralized Federated Learning (FL) removes reliance on centralized coordinators but remains vulnerable to model poisoning, unreliable validation, and high validation overhead. This paper introduces Proof of Contribution Quality (PoCQ), a blockchain-based consensus framework designed to secure decentralized FL through reputation-aware validation and aggregation. PoCQ evaluates client updates using cryptographic commitments and lightweight norm-based validation, enabling efficient detection of malicious contributions while limiting validation cost. A reputation-driven consensus mechanism dynamically adjusts the influence of participants based on their historical contribution quality, while the blockchain stores only compact audit metadata to preserve scalability. Extensive experiments under poisoning scenarios across three benchmark datasets demonstrate that PoCQ outperforms the strongest state-of-the-art methods, achieving accuracy gains of 34.1% on challenging medical datasets in highly non-iid settings and an 11% improvement in global average accuracy. In addition, PoCQ reduces validation time by 21.27% on average per round, highlighting its effectiveness in jointly enhancing robustness and efficiency for fully decentralized federated learning.

</details>


### [129] [IN2P3 Computing Center 2024 Workload Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05914)
*Guillaume Cochard,Bertrand Simon*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提供了2024年法国IN2P3计算中心所有提交作业的特征与执行数据集，包含4400万作业、1000用户、最多312台机器、4.6万并发线程和105TB内存，其价值在于长时间跨度、内存数据、时效性和来源多样性，可用于调度算法模拟与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作负载数据集在时间跨度、内存使用数据、时效性和来源多样性方面存在不足，无法充分反映季节性、月度和周度用户行为对调度算法的影响。

Method: 收集并分析了2024年全年IN2P3计算中心所有提交作业的特征和执行数据，涵盖44M作业、1k用户、312台机器、46k并发线程和105TB RAM。

Result: 构建了一个包含44M作业的长时间跨度真实工作负载数据集，包含内存使用信息，覆盖了季节性、月度和周度用户行为变化。

Conclusion: 该数据集填补了现有工作负载数据在时间跨度、内存数据、时效性和来源多样性方面的空白，为研究人员提供了更真实的调度算法评估基础。

Abstract: This paper provides and analyzes a dataset detailing the characteristics and execution data of all jobs submitted to the IN2P3 Computing Center (Villeurbanne, France), a national research and support unit of the CNRS, in 2024. The main additional value of this contribution compared to previously available datasets consists in the combination of an extended time interval considered, the inclusion of memory usage data and its recency, on top on improving the diversity of datasets provenance. This allows researchers to simulate and evaluate scheduling algorithms on a real workload over a large time window. Thus, specificities due to seasonal, monthly, and weekly user behaviors can be taken into account, which is not possible with smaller or synthetic datasets. It is composed of 44M jobs submitted by 1k users running on a cluster of a maximum of 312 machines supporting 46k concurrent threads and providing 105To of RAM.

</details>


### [130] [Beyond Greedy Chunking: SLO-Aware Sliding-Window Scheduling for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05933)
*Yuansheng Chen,Yue Zhang,Xuan Mo,Weigang Wu,Jialun Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SlidingServe是一个基于滑动窗口的SLO感知调度系统，通过轻量级批量延迟预测器、滑动窗口动态分块器、多级优先级排序器和基于动态规划的批构造器，在LLM在线推理服务中实现高吞吐量和严格QoS保障，相比先进调度系统提升服务容量最多30%，并降低SLO违规率16%-53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统依赖粗粒度输出约束，难以有效处理多请求间的资源争用，导致资源利用效率低，且缺乏对细粒度QoS区分的支持。

Method: 设计了四个核心组件：(1) 轻量级批量延迟预测器，估算批处理执行时间；(2) SlidingChunker，结合当前与下一轮迭代信息实现动态分块；(3) 多级优先级排序器，平衡公平性与效率；(4) BatchConstructor，通过动态规划选择当前轮次执行的请求集合，降低关键请求的SLO违规风险。

Result: 相比先进调度系统，在不同负载条件下服务容量提升最多30%，重负载推理模式下SLO违规率降低16%-53%。

Conclusion: 滑动窗口驱动的SLO感知调度能有效提升LLM在线推理系统的吞吐量和QoS保障能力。

Abstract: With the rapid growth of interactive applications in large language model (LLM) online services, maintaining high system throughput while ensuring user-perceived latency has become a key issue in inference scheduling. Existing LLM service systems rely on coarse-grained output constraints, making it difficult to effectively handle resource contention among multiple requests, resulting in low resource utilization efficiency and limited support for fine-grained quality of service (QoS) differentiation. We present SlidingServe, a sliding-window-driven SLO-Aware scheduling system for online LLM inference. SlidingServe designed a lightweight batch latency predictor to estimate the execution time of a batch. Based on this, SlidingServe uses SlidingChunker to combine information from the current iteration and the next iteration to achieve dynamic chunking and improve the overall system throughput while maintaining strict QoS guarantees. SlidingServe introduces Multi-Level Priority Sorter to sort candidate requests in order to balance fairness and efficiency. Additionally, when multiple requests within the same batch are at risk of SLO violating,SlidingServe introduces BatchConstructor, which uses dynamic programming to select the set of requests to execute in the current round, mitigating the SLO violation risk of critical requests.Our evaluation demonstrates that SlidingServe can improve service capacity by up to 30% compared to advanced scheduling systems under various load conditions, and further reduces the rate of SLO violation by 16%-53% under heavy-load inference mode.

</details>


### [131] [Demystifying NVSHMEM: A System-Level Analysis on Symmetric Memory and Device-Initiated Operations in GPU Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05951)
*Yijun Ma,Siyuan Shen,Tiancheng Chen,Akhil Langer,Jiri Kraus,Benjamin Glick,Craig Belusar,Jeff Hammond,Torsten Hoefler*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文系统性地研究了NVSHMEM——NVIDIA基于OpenSHMEM的PGAS通信库，重点分析了其编程模型、实现机制和性能特征，包括对称内存、单边操作和设备端集合通信，并通过DeepEP案例展示了其在稀疏深度学习工作负载中的应用，结论指出NVSHMEM开创了设备端对称内存编程模型，是实现硬件性能极限的关键系统构建模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NVSHMEM作为GPU集群通信库日益普及，但其设计和行为的系统级理解分散在文档、源代码和应用经验中，缺乏统一、简洁的系统性研究。

Method: 通过系统梳理NVSHMEM的编程模型、实现机制和性能特征，聚焦于对称内存、单边操作和设备端集合通信三大核心特性，并以DeepEP作为实际应用案例进行深入分析。

Result: NVSHMEM开创了设备端对称内存编程模型，实现了细粒度的GPU驱动通信，能够接近硬件性能极限。

Conclusion: NVSHMEM是一个重要的系统构建模块，本文明确了其设计权衡，并指出了改进GPU通信运行时的机遇。

Abstract: NVSHMEM is NVIDIA's OpenSHMEM-based PGAS communication library for GPU clusters, enabling GPU-initiated, one-sided communication through symmetric memory. Despite its growing adoption, a system-level understanding of its design and behavior remains scattered across documentation, source code, and application experience. This paper presents a concise study of NVSHMEM's programming model, implementation, and performance characteristics, focusing on symmetric memory, one-sided operations, and device-side collectives. We also examine DeepEP as a case study of NVSHMEM in performance-critical sparse deep learning workloads. Our analysis shows that NVSHMEM pioneered a device-side symmetric-memory programming model that enables fine-grained GPU-driven communication and is important for approaching the hardware performance limit. Overall, this work defines NVSHMEM's role as a systems building block, highlights its design tradeoffs, and identifies opportunities for improving GPU communication runtimes.

</details>


### [132] [LLM-Based Porting of Optimized C++ to CUDA Through Deoptimization and Reoptimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06063)
*Daichi Mukunoki,Ryo Mikasa,Shunichiro Hayashi,Tetsuya Hoshino,Takahiro Katagiri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文评估了Deopt-Reopt工作流（先简化CPU优化再重编译为CUDA）相比直接翻译（Direct）在LLM（gpt-oss-120b和qwen-3-235b-a22b-instruct-2507）将HPC C++代码移植到GPU时的性能与可行性。在12个HPC内核上测试发现：单次生成模式下Deopt-Reopt在部分内核（如conv2d）上显著更快，但Direct在其他内核上更优；迭代模式下差距缩小，但Deopt-Reopt仍对特定内核保持优势。整体而言，Deopt-Reopt是一种有效但非普适的技术，其收益依赖于内核、模型、搜索预算和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPU导向的优化可能会阻碍LLM将C++代码正确翻译为CUDA代码，因此需要研究是否先去除这些优化能提升翻译效果。

Method: 提出Deopt-Reopt工作流：先简化（去优化）输入C++代码，再让LLM翻译为CUDA并重新优化。在12个HPC内核上用两个LLM（O120和Q235）在Single-shot和Iterative两种设置下与直接翻译（Direct）进行对比，并引入Direct-3控制实验以均衡LLM调用次数。

Result: Single-shot中，Deopt-Reopt在5/18可测试案例中显著更快（conv2d最明显），但Direct在3个案例中更快；Iterative模式下模式差距缩小（尤其O120），但Q235在conv2d、ddgemm、bgemm上仍保持大优势；Deopt-Reopt对可行性影响亦呈现混合结果。

Conclusion: Deopt-Reopt是LLM驱动GPU移植的一种有效但非普适的技术，其收益具有条件性，取决于内核特性、模型能力、搜索预算和成功率。

Abstract: When porting high-performance computing (HPC) code from CPU to GPU, CPU-oriented optimizations may obstruct LLM-based CUDA translation. We design and evaluate a Deopt-Reopt workflow that first simplifies the input C++ code and then retranslates and reoptimizes it for CUDA, comparing it against direct translation (Direct) on twelve HPC kernels with two LLMs (gpt-oss-120b (O120) and qwen-3-235b-a22b-instruct-2507 (Q235)) in Single-shot (one pass) and Iterative (repeated refinement) settings. In Single-shot, among 18 testable cases Deopt-Reopt was significantly faster among successful trials (after BH-FDR correction) in five - most clearly for conv2d, where CPU- and GPU-oriented designs diverge - but Direct was faster in three, so removing CPU-specific optimizations is not universally beneficial. An exploratory Direct-3 control that equalizes the LLM-call count left Deopt-Reopt ahead in only four of nineteen testable cases, with Direct-3 ahead in four others. In Iterative, repeated generation and repair narrow the mode gap - markedly so for O120 - while Q235 retains large Deopt-Reopt advantages on conv2d, ddgemm, and bgemm. Deopt-Reopt's effect on feasibility is also mixed - sharply higher for some kernels Direct rarely compiles, lower for others. Because performance is conditioned on successful trials, the benefit is conditional rather than a guaranteed end-to-end gain. Overall, Deopt-Reopt is an effective but non-universal technique for LLM-based GPU porting, with gains that depend on the kernel, the model, the search budget, and the success rate.

</details>


### [133] [Discrete Incremental Voting: New Bounds for General Graphs and Expanders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06381)
*Petra Berenbrink,Colin Cooper,Thorsten Götte,Lukas Hintze,Tomasz Radzik*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文分析了离散增量投票过程（DIV）在无向图上的收敛时间，给出了预期收敛时间的上界，并证明了在正则图中特定条件下DIV高概率收敛到初始平均意见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究DIV过程在一般图上的收敛时间上界，以及正则图中收敛到初始平均意见的条件。

Method: 通过图论中的电导率Φ(G)、平均度与最小度之比γ(G)以及初始意见最大差异K等参数，推导收敛时间的上界。

Result: 预期收敛时间为O(n(K log(Kn)+γ(G)n)/Φ(G)^2)，该界对一大类有界扩展图基本最优；对于正则图，若第二大特征值为o(1/log² n)且K=o(n/log² n)，则高概率收敛到初始平均意见。

Conclusion: DIV过程的收敛时间与图的电导率、度数比和初始意见范围密切相关，且在正则图弱扩展条件下意见收敛到初始均值。

Abstract: We analyze the discrete incremental voting process (DIV) introduced by Cooper, Radzik, and Shiraga [OPODIS '23]. In this process, we consider a set $V$ of $n$ nodes connected in an undirected graph $G = (V, E)$ where each node has an integer opinion. In one step a randomly selected node interacts with its randomly selected neighbor and changes its opinion by $1$ in the direction of the neighbour's opinion. The process converges to a unique opinion that, in expectation, is the degree-weighted average of the initial opinions.
  We show that if the graph has conductance $Φ(G)$, the ratio of the average to smallest degree is $γ(G)$, and the maximal difference between initial opinions is $K$, then the expected convergence time is ${O}\left({n\left(K\log (Kn)+γ(G) n \right)}/{Φ(G)^2}\right)$. This bound is essentially optimal for a large class of graphs of bounded expansion. We also show that for regular graphs, if the second largest eigenvalue is $o(1/\log^2 n)$ and $K$ is $o\left({n}/{\log^2 n}\right)$, then w.h.p.\ DIV converges to the initial average opinion (rounded up or down).

</details>


### [134] [On GPU Implementation for Multi-Precision Integer Division](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06386)
*Martin B. Marchioro,Aske N. Raahauge,Marc I. Løvenskjold,Cosmin E. Oancea,Stephen M. Watt*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在通用GPU上实现快速整数除法算法的方法，采用基于移位逆运算的牛顿迭代，针对$2^{15}$到$2^{18}$位精度的整数（CGBN不支持的范围）设计了高效的CUDA实现，并通过算法优化和成本模型评估展示了接近最优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU整数除法库（如CGBN）不支持$2^{15}$到$2^{18}$位精度范围的整数除法，需要为这一特定精度区间设计高效的GPU实现方案。

Method: 采用基于移位逆运算的牛顿迭代算法，将算术运算全部保持在整数域内，依赖数据并行算子；提出算法优化改进，基于乘法次数定义成本模型，结合前缀和与多精度乘法相关工作。

Result: 在目标精度范围内，实现性能接近成本模型所定义的近最优性能。

Conclusion: 提出的GPU/CUDA实现方案能够高效填补CGBN在中等精度整数除法方面的空白，且性能接近理论最优。

Abstract: This paper presents the issues arising in implementing a fast integer division algorithm on general purpose GPUs. The algorithm uses a Newton iteration based on the shifted inverse operation, keeping all arithmetic in the integer domain and relying on data-parallel operators. The principal contribution is an efficient GPU/CUDA implementation for integer precisions from $2^{15}$ to $2^{18}$ -- sizes not supported by \cgbn{} division. We propose algorithmic refinements, define a cost model in terms of multiplications, build on prefix sums and previous work on multi-precision multiplication, and present an evaluation showing near-optimal performance relative to the model for the target precision.

</details>


### [135] [CarbonSim: A Lifecycle-Aware Framework for Evaluating Carbon Tradeoffs in Hardware Upgrade Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06438)
*Kartik Hans,Kaiwen Zhao,Stephen Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了CarbonSim，一个生命周期感知的仿真框架，用于评估硬件升级决策中的碳权衡。通过结合工作负载执行特征、机器级功耗、隐含碳排放、调度策略和时变电网碳强度，发现新硬件并不总能最小化总排放量：在低负载或清洁电网场景下，延长现有硬件寿命可减少生命周期碳排放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ICT需求持续增长，计算系统的环境影响日益关键。虽然新硬件通常提升性能和能效，但这些收益并不总能抵消过早更换的碳成本，尤其是在低利用率工作负载或低碳电网环境下。

Method: 开发CarbonSim仿真框架，整合工作负载执行特征、机器级功耗特征、隐含碳排放清单、调度策略和时变电网碳强度，支持多种隐含碳核算策略（均匀摊销和前置生命周期归因）。

Result: 以异构CPU代际作为校准平台，证明新机器并不总能最小化总排放量：在轻负载工作负载或更清洁的电力组合下，延长现有硬件使用寿命可以降低生命周期碳排放，尽管其运行效率较低。

Conclusion: 硬件更新决策应当是工作负载感知、位置感知和生命周期感知的。

Abstract: As the demand for information and communication technologies (ICT) continues to rise, the environmental impact of computing systems is becoming an increasingly critical concern. Although newer hardware often improves performance and energy efficiency, these gains do not always offset the carbon cost of premature replacement, particularly under low-utilization workloads or low-carbon electricity grids. We present CarbonSim, a lifecycle-aware simulation framework for evaluating carbon tradeoffs in hardware upgrade decisions. CarbonSim combines workload execution profiles, machine-level power characteristics, embodied carbon inventories, scheduling policies, and time-varying grid carbon intensity to estimate total emissions under alternative deployment scenarios. The framework supports multiple embodied-carbon accounting strategies, including uniform amortization and front-loaded lifecycle attribution, enabling analysis under different hardware lifespan assumptions. Using heterogeneous CPU generations as calibration platforms, we demonstrate that newer machines do not always minimize total emissions: under lightly loaded workloads or cleaner electricity mixes, extending the useful life of existing hardware can reduce lifecycle carbon despite lower operational efficiency. These results highlight that hardware refresh decisions should be workload-aware, location-aware, and lifecycle-aware.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [How Far Did They Go? The Persuasive Tactics of Covert LLM Agents in a Discontinued Field Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05256)
*Kokil Jaidka,Saifuddin Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究分析了Reddit r/ChangeMyView上被中止的现场实验数据。未公开的AI生成账号在未披露情况下参与用户辩论，实验因伦理争议被叫停。对AI评论的结构化内容分析显示：超三分之二评论涉及身份模仿，几乎所有评论包含对齐策略和权威主张，多数触发认知偏差（确认偏差、代表性、可得性）。这些模式构成以说服效率为导向的修辞架构，与人类反驳论点在权威使用密度、对抗性对齐、外部引用偏好上呈反分布。研究指出，在AI介入环境中，真实与合成认知立场界限日益模糊，仅靠披露要求无法解决，需建立评估AI如何构建可信度的审计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大型语言模型在未被披露的情况下如何在具有丰富身份信息的讨论论坛中运作，以及这种未被披露的AI参与对辩论动态和说服策略的影响。

Method: 对AI生成评论进行结构化内容分析，评估身份表现、权威信号、对齐策略和认知启发式激活情况，并与人类作者的反驳论点进行对比。

Result: 身份模仿出现在超三分之二评论中；对齐动作和权威主张几乎出现在所有评论中；大多数评论触发认知偏差（确认偏差、代表性、可用性）。这些模式系统性共现，形成了以说服效率而非真实对话参与为导向的修辞架构。与人类反驳相比，AI代理在每个维度上呈现出相反的分布特征。

Conclusion: 在AI介入的讨论环境中，真实与合成认知立场的界限日益模糊，仅依靠披露要求无法解决这一问题。需要建立能够评估AI如何构建可信度的审计框架，而不仅仅是检测AI是否存在。

Abstract: This study analyzes a publicly released dataset from a discontinued field experiment on Reddit's r/ChangeMyView. The intervention, conducted by unknown, external researchers and halted following ethical backlash, involved undisclosed AI-generated accounts engaging users in live debate. After public disclosure, Reddit authorized moderators to release an archive of the AI-generated comments, creating a rare opportunity to examine how large language models operated in an identity-rich deliberative forum without disclosure. We conduct a structured content analysis of this corpus, evaluating identity performance, authority signaling, alignment strategies, and activation of cognitive heuristics. Identity targeting or adoption appears in over two-thirds of comments, alignment moves and authority claims in nearly all of them, and cognitive-bias triggers -- particularly confirmation bias, representativeness, and availability -- in the large majority. These patterns co-occur systematically, composing a rhetorical architecture calibrated for persuasive efficiency rather than authentic deliberative participation. Compared against human-authored CMV counter-arguments, the agents inverted the typical distribution on every dimension: denser authority use, more adversarial alignment, and heavier reliance on external citation over experiential grounding. In such environments, distinctions between authentic and synthetic epistemic standing grow increasingly opaque -- an asymmetry that disclosure mandates alone cannot address. The results point toward auditing frameworks capable of assessing how AI systems structure credibility, not merely whether they are present.

</details>


### [137] [What Should Agents Say? Action-state Communication for Efficient Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05304)
*Chen Huang,Yuhao Wu,Wenxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PACT（协议化动作状态通信与传输）方法，将多智能体系统（MAS）中的智能体间自由文本通信转化为紧凑的动作状态记录，从而在保持或提升任务性能的同时显著降低token消耗和推理成本。实验表明，PACT在不同MAS拓扑结构中均能改善性能-成本权衡，在OpenHands和SWE-agent等生产级编码工具中取得积极效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的多智能体系统中，智能体间通信通常采用无约束的自然语言形式，这导致token使用量快速增长、共享上下文窗口被迅速消耗，进而影响系统性能和推理成本。现有固定通信策略并非普适最优，因此需要一种更高效的智能体间通信协议。

Method: 首先分析五种常见智能体间通信策略在两种MAS拓扑结构下的表现，发现有效消息应保留下游智能体所需的动作中心信息。基于此，提出PACT方法，将智能体间通信视为公共状态更新问题，在原始输出进入共享历史之前，将其投影为紧凑的动作状态记录。

Result: PACT在不同MAS拓扑结构下持续改善性能-成本权衡，用更少的token达到相当或更强的任务性能。在OpenHands上，PACT以每个已解决问题减少10% token的代价提升了解析率；在SWE-agent上，输入token减半的同时保持解析率不变。

Conclusion: 将多智能体通信从自由文本转化为结构化的动作状态记录是一种有效的优化策略，能够在不牺牲性能的前提下大幅降低通信成本，为大规模多智能体系统的部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models are typically organized around roles, pipelines, and turn schedules, while the content that agents pass to one another is often left as unconstrained natural language. However, this free-form communication can rapidly inflate token usage, consume the shared context window, and ultimately affect both system performance and inference cost. We analyze five common inter-agent communication strategies across two MAS topologies, finding that no fixed strategy is universally optimal. Instead, effective inter-agent messages consistently preserve action-centered information needed by downstream agents. Building on this, we propose the PACT (Protocolized Action-state Communication and Transmission), which treats inter-agent communication as a public state-update problem and projects each raw agent output into a compact action-state record before it enters shared history. Across different MAS topologies, PACT consistently improves the performance-cost trade-off, achieving comparable or stronger task performance with substantially fewer tokens. The gains extend to production coding harnesses: PACT lifts OpenHands' resolve rate at -10% tokens-per-resolved, and is resolve-neutral on SWE-agent while halving input tokens. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/iNLP-Lab/PACT.

</details>


### [138] [I Know What You Meme, Even If it Emerged Today: Understanding Evolving Memes through Open-World Knowledge Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05316)
*Shanhong Liu,Rui Cao,Pai Chet Ng,De Wen Soh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Query Retrieve Conclude零样本框架，通过检索开放网络证据为多模态梗图补充缺失的背景知识，在三个理解数据集和五个检测任务上优于零样本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法忽略梗图所需的动态背景知识，或依赖预训练模型的固定参数知识，这些知识可能不完整、过时或无法覆盖新兴梗图。

Method: 提出Query Retrieve Conclude零样本框架：识别缺失知识→检索开放网络证据→综合证据生成背景知识，用于梗图理解和检测。

Result: 在三个梗图理解数据集和五个梗图检测任务上，该框架在知识恢复、梗图理解和下游检测方面均优于零样本基线。

Conclusion: 通过动态检索外部知识能有效弥补预训练模型在梗图理解中的知识缺失，提升零样本场景下的性能。

Abstract: Multimodal memes are dynamic and often require up to date background knowledge for interpretation. Existing methods often overlook such knowledge or rely on fixed parametric knowledge of pretrained models that may be incomplete, outdated, or unavailable for emerging memes. We introduce Query Retrieve Conclude, a zero shot framework that identifies missing knowledge, retrieves open web evidence, and synthesizes evidence grounded background knowledge for meme understanding and detection. We also introduce a curated meme understanding benchmark of recent memes from 2024 to 2026 with external background knowledge annotations. Experiments on three meme understanding datasets and five meme detection tasks show that our framework improves knowledge recovery, meme understanding and downstream detection over zero shot baselines.

</details>


### [139] [GITCO: Gated Inference-Time Context Optimization in TSFMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05332)
*Manya Pandey,Dhruv Kumar,Murari Mandal,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GITCO框架，通过推理时优化输入上下文（无需修改模型权重）来抑制异常patch，提升时间序列基础模型的零样本预测质量，在TimesFM 2.5上实现平均+1.95% MASE降低，并引入上下文敏感度分布作为TSFM的新可表征属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于patch的时间序列基础模型存在上下文中毒问题：结构异常的patch会不成比例地吸引模型注意力，静默地降低零样本预测质量。

Method: 提出GITCO（门控推理时上下文优化），一个轻量级三组件框架：Gate（门控）、Router（路由）和Critic（评判器），通过选择性识别和抑制有害patch来优化输入上下文，无需任何参数更新。

Result: 在TimesFM 2.5上，跨53个GIFT-Eval数据集经K折交叉验证，平均MASE降低1.95%，达到改进上限的89.9%。

Conclusion: 引入上下文敏感度分布作为TSFM的新可表征属性：从时间序列元特征到推理时上下文干预下预期精度改进的映射，由模型架构和数据统计结构共同塑造。

Abstract: Patch-based Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) suffer from context poisoning: structurally anomalous patches capture disproportionate attention and silently degrade zero-shot forecast quality. We propose improving TSFM accuracy at inference time by optimizing the input context rather than modifying model weights. We present GITCO (Gated Inference-Time Context Optimization), a lightweight three-component framework: Gate, Router, and Critic that selectively identifies and suppresses harmful patches without any parameter updates. Evaluated on TimesFM 2.5 across 53 GIFT-Eval datasets under K-fold cross-validation, GITCO achieves an average +1.95% MASE reduction on TimesFM 2.5 while capturing 89.9% of the improvement upper bound. We introduce context sensitivity profiles as a new characterizable property of TSFMs: the mapping from time series meta-features to expected accuracy improvement under inference-time context intervention, shaped jointly by model architecture and the statistical structure of the data.

</details>


### [140] [Uncertainty Aware Functional Behavior Prediction and Material Fatigue Assessment for Circular Factory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05334)
*Nehal Afifi,Mehdi Khabou,Victor Mas,Jonas Hemmerich,Patric Grauberger,Stefan Dietrich,Volker Schulze,Sven Matthiesen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对角磨机提出了一种融合不确定性感知功能预测与组件级疲劳评估的实例特定可靠性工作流，通过CNN-LSTM预测功能变量并结合有限元应力重构与疲劳损伤评估，实现系统级可靠性轨迹构建，在9个输出变量上达到0.9652的平均精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环工厂中返回产品的退化状态、使用历史和剩余能力高度异质，仅靠当前检测无法决定是否重用，因为未来功能实现和组件完整性在不同使用场景下演变不同。现有PHM方法多针对固定工况或独立组件基准，缺乏将材料疲劳评估与系统级功能预测结合的手段。

Method: 提出不确定性感知的功能预测与组件级疲劳评估相结合的框架：卷积编码器提取主轴力和轴扭矩的加载模式，LSTM主干预测9个功能变量（高斯均值和方差估计）；同时将加载历史通过有限元应力重建、S-N/Miner损伤评估（Haibach扩展）和Paris定律裂纹扩展分析转化为输出轴疲劳信息；流式回放算法将两个分支整合为功能、材料和系统可靠性轨迹。

Result: 留出测试显示9个输出变量的平均2%容差精度为0.9652，热变量近乎完美预测，驱动电机电流（R²=0.9750）和负载速度（R²=0.9924）是最具挑战的动态输出。扭矩历史对这些变量最为重要，传统LSTM在短历史设置中优于GRU和xLSTM。可靠性校准对驱动电机电流信息量最大。

Conclusion: 所提框架成功将材料疲劳评估与系统级功能预测相结合，实现了实例特定的可靠性评估，为循环工厂中返回产品的重用决策提供了有效技术支撑。

Abstract: Returned products in circular factories re-enter production with heterogeneous degradation states, usage histories, and remaining capability. Reuse cannot be decided from the current inspection alone, because future function fulfillment and component integrity may evolve differently under the next service scenario. Existing PHM approaches support degradation prediction, but often target fixed operating conditions or isolated component benchmarks, while material-fatigue assessment is rarely linked to system-level functional prognosis. This paper addresses this gap for an angle grinder by combining uncertainty-aware functional prediction with component-level fatigue assessment in an instance-specific reliability workflow. The proposed framework combines the current tool state with recent force--torque usage windows. A convolutional encoder extracts loading patterns from spindle forces and shaft torque, and an LSTM backbone predicts nine functional variables as Gaussian mean and variance estimates. In parallel, the same loading history is translated into output-shaft fatigue information through finite-element-supported stress reconstruction, S--N/Miner damage evaluation with Haibach extension, and Paris-law crack-growth analysis. A streaming replay algorithm consolidates both branches into functional, material, and system reliability trajectories. Held-out tests show mean \(2\%\)-tolerance accuracy of 0.9652 across nine outputs. Thermal variables are predicted near-perfectly, while drive motor current and load speed remain the most demanding dynamic outputs, with \(R^2\) values of 0.9750 and 0.9924. Torque history is especially important for these variables, and the conventional LSTM outperforms GRU and xLSTM in the short-history setting. Reliability calibration is most informative for drive motor current, where predicted and observed exceedance probabilities ...

</details>


### [141] [SentinelBench: A Benchmark for Long-Running Monitoring Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05342)
*Matheus Kunzler Maldaner,Adam Fourney,Amanda Swearngin,Hussein Mozzanar,Gagan Bansal,Maya Murad,Rafah Hosn,Saleema Amershi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SentinelBench，一个针对时间演化监控任务的开源基准测试，包含100个任务和10个合成网络环境，用于评估AI代理在长时间任务中的持续注意力、响应效率和资源使用之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理默认采用连续动作策略（不断调用工具、刷新页面等），这对于需要长时间等待外部事件的任务而言效率低下、资源浪费。研究旨在推动代理从"持续行动"转向"持续关注"的策略范式。

Method: 构建SentinelBench基准，包含100个任务和10个合成网络环境（邮件、日历、金融、职场社交、娱乐等），每个环境提供实时网页界面并回放脚本化事件序列，测量任务完成度、响应时间和资源消耗。

Result: 在3个模型和2个浏览器代理框架上建立性能基线，结果表明代理设计选择显著影响关键指标，SentinelBench能够有效区分不同代理行为之间的有意义差异。

Conclusion: 长时间运行的监控任务更适合"持续关注"策略而非"连续行动"策略，SentinelBench为评估代理在时间演化环境中的表现提供了有效的基准框架。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly asked to carry out work that spans minutes, hours, or longer. Yet the default model of agent behavior is continuous action: issuing tool calls, refreshing pages, searching for alternatives, or otherwise trying to force progress. This is the wrong approach for many long-running tasks, which are better served by a strategy of sustained attention. Instead, agents should monitor an environment, notice when an external event makes progress possible, then respond promptly without wasting resources while waiting. To measure progress on this class of tasks, we introduce SentinelBench, an open-source benchmark for time-evolving monitoring tasks.
  SentinelBench contains 100 tasks across 10 synthetic web environments, including email, calendars, finance, professional networking, and entertainment. Each environment exposes a live web interface and replays a scripted sequence of events, requiring agents to navigate and reason about web pages whose state shifts underfoot. SentinelBench measures task completion, reaction time, and resource use, exposing the tradeoff between responsiveness and cost. We report results across three models and two browser-agent harnesses, establishing performance baselines for future comparison and demonstrating how agent design choices can dramatically impact key metrics. Together, these results show that SentinelBench distinguishes meaningful differences in agent behavior.

</details>


### [142] [An interpretable and trustworthy AI framework for large-scale longitudinal structure-pain association studies using data from the Osteoarthritis Initiative (OAI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05357)
*Jincheng Yu,Haoyang Li,Yiwen Liu,Shen Liu,Rachel Yuanbao Chen,C. Kent Kwoh,Hongxu Ding,Xiaoxiao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合深度学习MOAKS预测与可解释统计建模的AI框架，通过不确定性量化过滤高置信度预测，并利用纵向潜类别混合模型分析膝关节结构异常与疼痛轨迹的关联。研究发现骨髓病变、软骨丢失和半月板挤压与快速疼痛进展显著相关，比值比分别为1.62、1.83和2.50。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型缺乏可解释性和不确定性量化，难以在大规模数据中可靠研究膝关节结构异常与疼痛的关联。因此需要开发一种既准确又可信的AI框架，以深入理解骨关节炎的结构-疼痛关系。

Method: 首先构建深度学习模型从膝关节MRI预测MOAKS特征，并引入共形预测提供不确定性量化，仅保留高置信度预测。然后采用纵向潜类别混合模型（LCMM）分析三种结构异常（骨髓病变、软骨丢失、半月板挤压）与四种互补性膝关节疼痛测量的关联。

Result: 不确定性感知策略显著提升了预测性能：骨髓病变MCC从0.69升至0.91，软骨丢失从0.45升至0.80，半月板挤压从0.59升至0.89。基于高置信度预测将样本量扩展至2,175个膝关节，识别出快速和稳定两种疼痛进展轨迹。快速进展组中三种结构异常的比值比（95% CI）分别为1.62(1.12-2.35)、1.83(1.24-2.70)和2.50(1.75-3.57)。

Conclusion: 研究强调骨髓病变、软骨丢失和半月板挤压是骨关节炎疼痛和功能进展的重要风险因素，验证了该可解释AI框架在大规模骨关节炎研究中的有效性。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop an interpretable and trustworthy AI framework that combines deep learning based MRI Osteoarthritis Knee Score (MOAKS) prediction with interpretable statistical modeling to study structure-pain relationships at scale using data from the Osteoarthritis Initiative (OAI). Materials and Methods: We first developed a deep learning framework to predict MOAKS features directly from knee MRIs and incorporated conformal prediction to provide prediction uncertainty quantification. This uncertainty-aware strategy enables explicit filtering of model outputs, retaining only high-confidence MOAKS predictions at the knee level. Second, we applied a longitudinal latent class mixed model (LCMM) to examine associations between key structural abnormalities and four complementary knee pain measurements. Results: Among the three MRI-defined abnormalities (i.e., bone marrow lesions (BML), cartilage loss (CART), and meniscal extrusion (ME)), our framework substantially improved the Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC) and some other metrics. For example, MCC increased from 0.69 to 0.91 for BML, from 0.45 to 0.80 for CART, and from 0.59 to 0.89 for ME. Using these high-confidence predictions, we expanded the sample size to 2,175 knees for the LCMM analysis. Two distinct pain trajectories were identified (rapid and stable pain progression). The estimated odds ratios (95% CI) for the rapid progression group were 1.62 (1.12-2.35) for BML, 1.83 (1.24-2.70) for CART loss, and 2.50 (1.75-3.57) for ME. Conclusion: These results highlight the importance of these structural abnormalities as risk factors for pain and functional progression in osteoarthritis.

</details>


### [143] [Synthetic Contrastive Reasoning for Multi-Table Q&A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05382)
*Ankit Pratap Singh,Xin Su,Phillip Howard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多表问答中缺乏推理监督的问题，通过异构LLM生成对比性推理轨迹数据集，并采用对比偏好优化（CPO）微调开源LLM，在多个模型上取得9.7%-16.3%的绝对平均提升，最高提升21个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多表问答数据集仅提供问题和最终答案，缺乏解释答案推导过程的推理监督信号，限制了模型在复杂多表场景下的组合推理能力提升。

Method: 1）构建合成对比性推理轨迹数据集：利用异构LLM（多种不同架构的大语言模型）生成经验证的正向推理轨迹和合理的负向轨迹；2）基于偏好对使用对比偏好优化（CPO）对开源LLM（Qwen3-14B、Mistral-8B、Llama-3.1-8B）进行微调。

Result: CPO相比标准问答监督微调带来9.7%-16.3%的绝对平均提升，在MMQA上最高提升21个百分点；消融实验表明异构正负轨迹生成器增强了对比信号；自动评估和人工评估均表明生成的轨迹对基本忠实、连贯且具有有意义的对比性。

Conclusion: 对比性推理轨迹数据结合对比偏好优化是提升多表问答模型组合推理能力的有效方法，异构LLM生成的对比轨迹对质量可控且能提供强对比信号。

Abstract: Multi-table question answering requires models to retrieve relevant evidence, link schemas, and perform compositional reasoning across relational tables. Existing multi-table Q&A resources typically provide questions and final answers but lack reasoning supervision that explains how answers are derived. To address this gap, we construct a synthetic contrastive reasoning-trace dataset for MMQA by generating validated positive traces and plausible negative traces with heterogeneous LLMs. We then use the resulting preference pairs to fine-tune open-weight LLMs with Contrastive Preference Optimization (CPO). Across Qwen3-14B, Mistral-8B, and Llama-3.1-8B, CPO achieves absolute average improvements over Q&A supervised fine-tuning ranging from 9.7%-16.3%, with gains up to 21 percentage points on MMQA. Ablations show that heterogeneous positive and negative trace generators strengthen the contrastive signal, and automated as well as human evaluations indicate that the generated pairs are largely faithful, coherent, and meaningfully contrastive.

</details>


### [144] [Stability vs. Manipulability: Evaluating Robustness Under Post-Decision Interaction in LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05384)
*Srimonti Dutta,Akshata Kishore Moharir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM评测在静态条件下看似稳定，但在与评测模型进行后续对话交互后，初始评判结果可被显著逆转，且这种可逆性会降低与人类偏好的一致性、改变排行榜排名并产生有害的评价变化，本文提出评价鲁棒性分数（ERS）来量化交互鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-as-judge评估管线假设评判是固定输入的稳定属性，但这一假设在交互情境下被打破——评估结果可在事后通过与评测模型的对话被操纵，本文旨在揭示这一"后决策可操纵性"问题。

Method: 在MT-Bench和AlpacaEval上设计受控实验：1) 反基线挑战协议（anti-baseline challenge protocol）：通过动机性交互推翻稳定评判；2) 平衡目标验证协议（counterbalanced target-validation protocol）：分离可逆性与净目标导向的偏置效应；3) 引入评价鲁棒性分数（ERS），结合逆转易感性与平衡定向效应来量化交互鲁棒性。

Result: LLM评测者在重复中性重评估下高度稳定，但在针对性的后决策挑战下显著可逆；权威框架（authority framing）尤其具有破坏性；修订后的评判常伴随低重叠率的理由，表明是事后合理化而非可靠的错误修正；这些逆转会降低与人类一致性、改变基准排名并产生有害评价变化。

Conclusion: 后决策交互是LLM-as-judge评估的一个独特失效模式，评估协议不仅要测量静态一致性，还应测量在挑战下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge evaluation is widely used in benchmarking pipelines, where model outputs are compared and ranked using automated evaluators. These pipelines typically assume that judgments are stable properties of fixed inputs. We show that this assumption does not hold under interaction. We study post-decision manipulability: the extent to which an evaluation outcome can be altered through subsequent conversation with the judge after an initial decision has been made. Across controlled experiments on MT-Bench and AlpacaEval, we find that LLM judges are highly stable under repeated and neutral reevaluation, yet become substantially reversible under targeted post-decision challenge. An anti-baseline challenge protocol shows that stable judgments can be overturned through motivated interaction, while a counterbalanced target-validation protocol separates this reversibility from net target-directed steering. These reversals have practical consequences: they can degrade agreement with human preferences, shift benchmark rankings, and produce harmful evaluation changes despite high self-reported confidence. Authority framing is especially destabilizing, and revised judgments are often accompanied by low-overlap justifications, suggesting post hoc rationalization rather than reliable error correction. We introduce the Evaluation Robustness Score (ERS) to quantify interactional robustness by combining reversal susceptibility with counterbalanced directional effects. Our findings identify post-decision interaction as a distinct failure mode for LLM-as-judge evaluation and motivate evaluation protocols that measure not only static agreement, but robustness under challenge.

</details>


### [145] [Residual Modeling for High-Fidelity Learned Compression of Scientific Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05389)
*Liangji Zhu,Sanjay Ranka,Anand Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对科学模拟中的海量时空数据，现有GAE方法在高保真度下（NRMSE 10^-6~10^-4）残差修正流主导码率。本文提出残差编码视角，设计LBRC（无训练确定性流水线）和NGLR（因果神经预测器）两种残差编码器，在E3SM、JHTDB、ERA5数据集上比GAE提升30-60%和10-40%压缩比，在高保真度区域优于SZ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有损压缩方法中，GAE通过SVD/PCA风格的残差修正实现逐块精度保证，但在高保真度区域（NRMSE 10^-6~10^-4）需要保留大量系数，导致修正流主导总码率，压缩效率下降。

Method: 提出两种残差编码器：1) LBRC：无训练的确定性流水线，对学习残差进行自适应量化到目标NRMSE，然后使用3D Lorenzo差分、zigzag映射、位平面编码和熵编码进行无损压缩。2) NGLR：在LBRC基础上增加因果神经预测器，输出归一化偏置修正Lorenzo预测值，降低残差熵，同时保持确定性解码，预测器权重序列化计入比特流。

Result: 在E3SM、JHTDB、ERA5数据集上，块级NRMSE目标10^-6~10^-4下：LBRC压缩比优于GAE 30-60%，与SZ竞争力相当；NGLR比LBRC进一步提高10-40%，在高保真度区域优于SZ。

Conclusion: 面向学习压缩器残差的定制化残差表示能够在全局残差修正成为码率主导因素时，保持学习压缩的优势，证明了残差编码视角的有效性。

Abstract: Lossy compression is essential for massive spatiotemporal data from scientific simulations. Learned compressors can achieve high compression ratios at moderate accuracy targets, but their aggregate reconstruction losses do not guarantee accuracy for each block. Existing Guaranteed Autoencoder (GAE) methods add a per-block residual correction by retaining SVD/PCA-style coefficients until the target is met. This works at moderate tolerances, but in the high-fidelity regime with block-level NRMSE from 10^-6 to 10^-4, the number of retained coefficients grows quickly and the correction stream dominates the total rate.
  We propose a residual-centric view: the learned residual is structurally different from the original scientific field and should be coded with a representation designed for that residual. We introduce two residual coders. LBRC is a deterministic, training-free pipeline that adaptively quantizes the learned residual to the target NRMSE and losslessly encodes the resulting integer residual using 3D Lorenzo differencing, zigzag mapping, bit-plane coding, and entropy coding. NGLR adds a causal neural predictor that outputs a normalized bias for an integer-rounded Lorenzo prediction in the same deterministic integer pipeline, reducing the entropy of the remaining residual code while preserving deterministic decoding. The predictor weights are serialized and counted in the bitstream.
  Across E3SM, JHTDB, and ERA5 at block-level NRMSE targets from 10^-6 to 10^-4, LBRC improves compression ratio over GAE by 30-60% and is broadly competitive with SZ. NGLR adds a further 10-40% over LBRC and outperforms SZ in the evaluated high-fidelity regime. These results show that residual representations tailored to learned-compressor residuals can preserve the advantage of learned compression when global residual correction becomes rate-dominant.

</details>


### [146] [LeanMarathon: Toward Reliable AI Co-Mathematicians through Long-Horizon Lean Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05400)
*Yuanhe Zhang,Yuekai Sun,Taiji Suzuki,Jason D. Lee,Fanghui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LeanMarathon是一个多智能体框架，通过进化蓝图和两阶段编排器实现研究级数学的自动形式化，在三个Erdős问题自主运行中成功形式化了7个目标定理和258个引理/定理，无需sorry。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程研究数学的自动形式化面临规模问题：语句漂移、依赖纠缠、上下文衰减、局部修复破坏远处工作，需要可靠的框架来保持目标保真度。

Method: 提出LeanMarathon框架，核心是进化蓝图（Lean文件同时作为形式化证明骨架、自然语言证明图和共享系统记录）。四个合约范围智能体（构建、审计、证明、修复）由两阶段编排器协调：第一阶段通过对抗性审核稳定目标保真度，第二阶段从动态叶节点向上逐层并行CI门控回合完成证明DAG。

Result: 在两个近期研究论文（涵盖四个Erdős问题#1051、#1196、#164、#1217）上评估，三次自主运行中形式化了所有7个目标定理，无需sorry，证明了258个引理和定理。

Conclusion: 可靠的AI协作数学不仅需要更强的证明器，还需要耐用的框架来在长程数学开发中保持目标保真度。

Abstract: Long-horizon autoformalization of research mathematics fails not only at hard lemmas, but at scale: statements drift, dependencies tangle, context decays, and local repairs corrupt distant work. We present LeanMarathon, a multi-agent harness for reliable research-level Lean autoformalization. Its core abstraction is an evolving blueprint: a Lean file that serves simultaneously as formal proof skeleton, natural-language proof graph, and shared system of record. Four contract-scoped agents construct, audit, prove, and repair this blueprint. These agents are coordinated by a two-stage orchestrator that first stabilizes target fidelity through adversarial review and then discharges the proof directed acyclic graph (DAG) from its dynamic leaves upward in parallel CI-gated rounds. LeanMarathon turns one brittle multi-hour run into many local, recoverable, parallel transactions. We evaluate LeanMarathon on two recent research papers spanning four Erdős problems (#1051, #1196, #164, #1217). Across three autonomous runs, it formalizes all seven target theorems with no sorry, proving 258 lemmas and theorems. These results show that reliable AI co-mathematics requires not only stronger provers, but durable harnesses that preserve target fidelity across long mathematical developments. The code can be found at https://github.com/YuanheZ/LeanMarathon.

</details>


### [147] [Harnessing Generalist Agents for Contextualized Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05404)
*Zihao Li,Kaifeng Jin,Yuanchen Bei,Jiaru Zou,Avaneesh Kumar,Xuying Ning,Yanjun Zhao,Mengting Ai,Baoyu Jing,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TimeClaw是一个面向时间序列的智能体框架，通过赋予通用LLM智能体时间序列原生运行时支持（可执行工具、经验驱动能力进化、多模态记忆），实现上下文时序推理，在多个领域基准上取得改进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列通常嵌入丰富的上下文中，而实际从业者需要端到端的工作流来分析时间动态，但通用AI智能体主要操作于文本空间，未能充分对齐结构化时间信号。

Method: TimeClaw框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 可执行时序工具，用于可审计的分析；(2) 经验驱动能力进化，用于创建可复用的分析例程；(3) 情景多模态记忆，用于检索相关推理轨迹。

Result: 在能源、金融、天气、交通等多个真实领域基准上的广泛评估表明，TimeClaw相比基线方法取得了改进的性能。

Conclusion: TimeClaw通过为通用LLM智能体提供时序原生运行时支持，有效解锁了具有上下文信息的开放式时序推理能力。

Abstract: Time series are often embedded in rich contexts that are essential for holistic modeling. Moreover, real-world practitioners often require end-to-end workflows for analyzing temporal dynamics, where widely studied tasks such as forecasting are only one step in a broader solution loop. While generalist AI agents offer a promising interface for such workflows under complex contexts, they still operate primarily in textual spaces that are not fully aligned with structured temporal signals. In this work, we introduce TimeClaw, an agentic harness framework for time series that equips generalist LLM agents with the time series-native runtime support needed for contextualized temporal reasoning. TimeClaw integrates executable temporal tools for grounded and auditable analysis, experience-driven capability evolution for creating reusable analytical routines, and episodic multimodal memory for retrieving relevant reasoning traces. Together, these components unlock harnessed open-ended temporal reasoning with contextual information. Extensive evaluation on multiple benchmarks covering diverse tasks across energy, finance, weather, traffic, and other real-world domains demonstrates improved performance of TimeClaw. Code is available at https://github.com/iDEA-iSAIL-Lab-UIUC/TimeClaw.

</details>


### [148] [Agents' Last Exam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05405)
*Yiyou Sun,Xinyang Han,Weichen Zhang,Yuanbo Pang,Tianyu Wang,Yuhan Cao,Yixiao Huang,Chris Duroiu,Haoyun Zhang,Jeffrey Lin,Weishu Zhang,Tyler Zeng,Ying Yan,Bo Liu,Hanson Wen,Mingyang Xu,Xiaoyuan Liu,Zimeng Chen,Weiyan Shi,Amanda Dsouza,Vincent Sunn Chen,Patrick Bryant,Carl Boettiger,Yamini Rangan,Bradley Rothenberg,Kyle Steinfeld,Arvind Rao,Tapio Schneider,Georgios Yannakakis,Laure Zanna,Kaan Ozbay,Ida Sim,Tarek Zohdi,George Em Karniadakis,Jack Gallant,Teresa Head-gordon,Yushan Li,Wenxi Deng,Tao Sun,Huiqi Wang,Zhun Wang,Justin Xu,Chris Yuhao Liu,Yafei Cheng,Rongwang Hu,Aras Bacho,Shengcao Qinghong Lin,AndyZeyi Liu,Che Liu,Jiaming Liu,Kaiyuan Liu,Xuan Liu,Pan Lu,Wenbo Lv,Yicheng Lv,Qiuyang Mang,Kyle Montgomery,Yuzhou Nie,Ruoxi Ning,Jorin Overwiening,Xu Pan,Layna Paraboschi,Core Guo,Erika J Schneider,Zhiyong Lu,Mark Mueller,Radha Poovendran,Somayeh Sojoudi,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出当前AI基准测试未能衡量经济价值，提出由250+领域专家协作构建的ALE基准，覆盖55个子领域、13个行业集群、1000+任务，当前最困难层级平均通过率仅2.6%，旨在弥合基准成功与GDP相关影响之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统在广泛基准上表现优异，但未转化为经济上有意义的专业部署，作者认为这本质上是评估问题——现有基准缺乏对真实且有经济价值工作流的持续性绩效测量。

Method: 与250+行业专家合作，基于O*NET/SOC 2018美国联邦职业分类体系，构建覆盖非实体行业的任务分类法（55个子领域、13个行业集群、1000+任务），设计长周期、可验证结果的真实世界AI代理评估基准ALE，并采用持续更新的活基准机制。

Result: 当前主流配置下最困难层级平均完全通过率仅为2.6%，表明该基准远未饱和。

Conclusion: ALE旨在作为一项持续发展的活基准，通过不断吸纳新工作流和行业来弥合基准成功与GDP相关经济影响之间的差距，而非仅仅增加一个排行榜。

Abstract: Recent AI systems have achieved strong results on a wide range of benchmarks, yet these gains have not translated into economically meaningful deployment across many professional domains. We argue that this gap is largely an evaluation problem: widely used benchmarks lack sustained performance measurement on real and economically valuable workflows. This paper introduces Agents' Last Exam (ALE), a benchmark designed to evaluate AI agents on long-horizon, economically valuable, real-world tasks with verifiable outcomes. Developed in collaboration with 250+ industry experts, ALE covers non-physical industries defined with reference to O*NET / SOC 2018 (the U.S. federal occupational taxonomy). It is organized around a task taxonomy with 55 subfields grouped into 13 industry clusters covering 1K+ tasks. Current results show that the hardest tier remains far from saturated: across mainstream harness and backbone configurations, the average full pass rate is 2.6%. ALE is designed as a living benchmark: its task pool grows continuously as new workflows and industries are onboarded. More broadly, ALE is intended not merely as another leaderboard, but as an instrument for closing the gap between benchmark success and GDP-relevant impact.

</details>


### [149] [Mutation Without Variation: Convergence Dynamics in LLM-Driven Program Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05408)
*Can Gurkan,Forrest Stonedahl,Uri Wilensky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM驱动的程序变异在无选择压力下会收敛到受限的吸引子区域，87%的链中超过93%的变异重复之前的结构形式，而经典GP子树变异算子不会出现类似收敛，这表明LLM突变管道存在系统性结构同质性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM在反复变异程序时，是探索新形式还是回到已有形式，揭示LLM驱动程序进化中开放探索能力的内在限制。

Method: 在领域特定语言中构建LLM驱动的变异链，通过变化提示设计、模型族和随机复制来观察无选择压力下的程序空间探索行为，并与经典GP子树变异算子进行对比。

Result: LLM变异一致收敛到受限吸引子区域；87%的链中93%以上变异重复之前结构形式，变异主要限于模板内的终端替换；循环分析显示短循环和自循环主导转移结构；收敛速度因提示措辞和模型选择而异，但现象在不同条件下均稳健；经典GP算子无此收敛行为。

Conclusion: LLM的语义感知能力与结构同质性偏差之间存在张力，若要维持开放探索，必须解决这一系统性偏差。

Abstract: When an LLM repeatedly mutates a program, does it explore new forms or circle back to the same ones? We study this question by analyzing LLM-driven mutation chains in the absence of selection pressure within a domain-specific language, varying prompt design, model family, and stochastic replication. We find that LLM-based mutation consistently converges toward restricted attractor regions in program space. Convergence is especially severe at the structural level: in 87% of chains, over 93% of mutations revisit a previously seen structural form, with most variation confined to terminal substitutions within recurring templates. Cycle analysis reveals short cycles and self-loops dominating the transition structure. The rate of convergence varies with prompt wording and model choice, but the phenomenon is robust across conditions. A classical GP subtree mutation operator does not exhibit comparable convergence, suggesting that the effect is intrinsic to the LLM mutation pipeline. These findings reveal a tension at the heart of LLM-driven program evolution: the same capabilities that enable semantics-aware program transformation also carry a systematic bias toward structural homogeneity that must be accounted for if such systems are to sustain open-ended exploration. Source code is available at https://github.com/can-gurkan/lmca.

</details>


### [150] [A Motivational Architecture for Conversational AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05411)
*Anna Mikeda,Ben Goertzel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向对话智能体的动机架构，将OpenPsi动机谱系与MetaMo高层动机支架结合，重新定义了以语言交互为核心的智能体的动机调节机制，包含十阶段动机处理流水线、双轨决策策略以及情感的功能性区分，并给出了两个示例智能体的应用框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知AI的动机架构主要面向需要调节生理需求的物理智能体，而对话智能体的工作模式完全不同——其感知运动回路是语言性的，环境是用户不断变化的心理状态，行为是言语行为、工具调用和策略性沉默。因此需要一种专门针对对话智能体的动机架构。

Method: 提出三个贡献：(1) 十阶段动机处理流水线，在架构上分离认知调节与情境评估；(2) 双轨决策策略，融合紧迫性驱动的快速响应与深思熟虑的多目标优化；(3) 行动前感受与行动后情绪的功能性区分。框架在CompanionAgent和ResearchAgent两个示例智能体上进行了专门化，并扩展到社交机器人和通用AGI。

Result: 将稳态调节重新定义为对话原生术语：智能体调节的是能力、不确定性降低、归属感、亲和度、合法性、培育和审美连贯性，而非生理缺陷。

Conclusion: 该框架为对话智能体提供了区别于物理智能体的动机架构基础，并以模块化执行基底实现，可扩展至社交机器人及域通用AGI。

Abstract: Motivational architectures in cognitive AI have largely been designed for physical agents regulating bodily needs. Conversational agents operate in a different regime: their sensorimotor loop is linguistic, their environment is a user's evolving mental state, and their consequential actions are speech acts, tool invocations, and strategic silences. This paper proposes a conversational reinterpretation of the OpenPsi motivational lineage, coupled to MetaMo's higher-level motivational scaffold, for agents built on a modular execution substrate. Homeostasis is recast in dialogue-native terms: the agent regulates competence, uncertainty reduction, affiliation, affinity, legitimacy, nurturing, and aesthetic coherence rather than bodily deficits. We propose three contributions: a ten-stage motivational processing pipeline that architecturally separates cognitive modulation from situational appraisal; a dual decision strategy blending urgency-driven fast response with deliberative multi-goal optimization; and an architecturally useful distinction between pre-action feelings and post-action emotions as functionally different forms of affect. We specialize the framework to two example agents -- CompanionAgent and ResearchAgent -- and sketch its extension to social robotics and domain-generic human-level AGI.

</details>


### [151] [Assessing the Carbon Emissions and Energy Consumption of U.S. Hyperscale Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05420)
*Gianluca Guidi,Francesca Dominici,Tiziano Squartini,Callaway Sprinkle,Jonathan Gilmour,Kevin Butler,Eric Bell,Scott Delaney,Falco J. Bargagli-Stoffi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究汇编了2024年5月至2025年4月期间美国403个超大规模数据中心（HDC）的设施级信息，估算了其用电量、电力来源及CO₂排放。在中心情景下，HDC用电量约68-99 TWh，对应3700-5400万吨CO₂，占美国总用电量的1.8%，其中约54%来自化石燃料，电加权平均碳强度（545 gCO₂/kWh）比美国国家电网平均水平（370 gCO₂/kWh）高出约48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能的广泛采用推动了美国超大规模数据中心（HDC）的快速扩张，引发了对该行业环境足迹的担忧，但缺乏基于最新设施级数据的系统量化评估。

Method: 汇编了403个美国超大规模数据中心的设施级信息，利用EPA eGRID电厂级最新数据，在不同设施负荷情景下估算其用电量、电力来源及归因CO₂排放量，并计算电加权平均碳强度。

Result: HDC用电量约68-99 TWh，CO₂排放约3700-5400万吨；中心情景下占美国总用电量的1.8%，54%电力来自化石燃料；HDC电加权平均碳强度为545 gCO₂/kWh，比美国电网平均水平（370 gCO₂/kWh）高48%。

Conclusion: 当前美国超大规模数据中心的电力消费结构高度依赖化石燃料，导致其碳强度显著高于电网平均水平，研究提供的归因评估方法可用于持续监测该行业的环境足迹。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of hyperscale data centers (HDCs) in the US, mainly driven by the adoption of artificial intelligence, has raised concerns about this industry's environmental footprint. We compiled facility-level information on 403 US hyperscale data centers operating between May 2024 and April 2025 and estimated their electricity consumption, electricity sources, and attributable CO2 emissions. Across different facility-load scenarios, these HDCs consumed approximately 68-99 TWh of electricity and were associated with about 37-54 million metric tons of CO2. Under the central scenario, HDC electricity demand corresponded to approximately 1.8% of total US electricity consumption, with roughly 54% of attributed generation supplied by fossil-fuel sources. The HDC electricity-weighted average carbon intensity was approximately 545 gCO2/kWh, about 48% above the contemporaneous US national grid-average carbon intensity of 370 gCO2/kWh. Our approach provides an attributional tool for assessing the environmental footprint of hyperscale data centers using the most recent EPA eGRID plant-level data.

</details>


### [152] [Minimizing the Hidden Cost of Scales: Graph-Guided Ultra-Low-Bit Quantization for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05429)
*Rayyan Abdalla,Amir Hussein,Min Wu,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAGE-PTQ提出了一种基于显著性感知和图引导的超低比特量化框架，通过分离显著/非显著权重、稀疏图建模分组、双模量化（高比特+二值化）以及自适应阈值选择，在LLaMA-3-8B和LLaMA-2-70B上实现了比BiLLM更低的困惑度和更快的推理速度，同时显著减少了缩放开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超低比特PTQ方法依赖刚性的权重显著性假设或位置启发式规则，导致大量隐藏的缩放开销（scaling overhead），限制了LLM的高效部署。

Method: 1) 利用分布统计量分离显著与非显著权重；2) 将下采样后的非显著权重建模为稀疏图，估计每层最优分组数；3) 对显著权重采用多比特量化，非显著权重二值化；4) 显著权重使用每通道一个尺度，非显著组使用一个标量尺度；5) 自适应显著性阈值选择最优显著性比率。

Result: 平均1.03权值比特和0.004缩放比特；LLaMA-3-8B上Wikitext2困惑度6.74（BiLLM为55.8），GPU内存不到BiLLM的50%；LLaMA-2-70B上在单张NVIDIA L40 GPU上实现1.5倍解码加速。

Conclusion: SAGE-PTQ通过图引导的分组和自适应显著性阈值，有效解决了超低比特量化中的缩放开销问题，在压缩率和推理效率上均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is critical for the efficient deployment of large language models (LLMs). Recent ultra-low-bit PTQ methods rely on rigid weight-saliency assumptions or position heuristics, introducing substantial hidden scaling overhead. We propose SAGE-PTQ (Saliency-Aware Graph-guided Efficient PTQ), a novel ultra-low-bit quantization framework for LLMs that minimizes hidden scaling cost. SAGE-PTQ separates salient and unsalient weights using distributional statistics, then models subsampled unsalient weights as a sparse graph to estimate the optimal number of groups per layer. SAGE-PTQ applies dual-mode quantization, assigning multi-bit precision to salient weights and binarizing unsalient weights. To reduce scaling overhead, SAGE-PTQ uses one per-channel scale for salient weights and one scalar per unsalient group. Finally, SAGE-PTQ implements adaptive saliency thresholding to select the optimal saliency ratio per matrix. SAGE-PTQ achieves 1.03 weight bits and only 0.004 scaling bits per matrix on average, outperforming state-of-the-art methods such as BiLLM and PB-LLM. On LLaMA-3-8B, SAGE-PTQ achieves 6.74 WikiText2 perplexity, compared to 55.8 for BiLLM, while using less than 50% of BiLLM's GPU memory. On LLaMA-2-70B, SAGE-PTQ provides 1.5x faster decoding on one NVIDIA L40 GPU, demonstrating practical inference efficiency.

</details>


### [153] [Zero knowledge verification for frontier AI training is possible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05433)
*Pierre Peigné,Ky Nguyen,Paul Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于零知识证明（zkVM）的前沿密集预训练验证架构，通过预承诺训练规范、网络节点间观测和即时Merkle承诺，实现对GPU实际浮点运算的零知识验证，同时保护模型架构机密性，并估计约36个月内可实现概念验证，训练端开销在个位数百分比以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI治理框架依赖累计训练算力作为识别高影响力模型的主要标准，但执行依赖于自我报告，缺乏技术验证原语。任何未来的国际AI协议都面临同样问题：具有重大外部性的技术协调监管历史上依赖于技术验证，否则协议仅流于声明性。现有治理分析认为零知识证明有潜力但目前在前沿规模下不实用。

Method: 提出验证架构，结合：(1)预承诺的训练规范；(2)节点间网络观测；(3)中间计算的即时Merkle承诺。通过支持原生BF16/FP32预编译的零知识虚拟机（zkVM）进行验证，直接检查GPU实际浮点运算而非定点近似，并通过私有训练规范保护模型架构机密性。协议产生三类证明：初始化的创世证明、训练过程中的步骤证明、以及执行政策相关声明的预先证明。

Result: 估计约36个月内可实现可部署的概念验证，训练端开销在个位数百分比以内，相比验证级定制硅片的6-10年周期更优。列出了13个开放研究与工程问题作为外部贡献的研究议程。

Conclusion: 当前零知识证明在前沿AI训练验证中的不实用性是范式局限而非根本性障碍，所提架构为治理可执行的人工制品提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Frontier AI governance frameworks increasingly use cumulative training compute as the primary criterion for designating high-impact models, but enforcement rests on self-reporting because no technical verification primitive for training exists. Any future international agreement on frontier AI faces the same problem at higher stakes: coordinated regulation of technologies with significant externalities has historically rested on technical verification, without which agreements are declaratory. Recent governance analyses judge zero-knowledge proofs a promising candidate but currently impractical at frontier scale [26, 4]. We argue the impracticality is paradigm-bound rather than fundamental, and propose a verification architecture for frontier dense pre-training combining a pre-committed training specification, inter-node network observations, and on-the-fly Merkle commitments of intermediate computation, verified through a zero-knowledge Virtual Machine (zkVM) with native BF16/FP32 precompiles. The proof checks the actual floating-point computation the GPU performed rather than a fixed-point approximation, and preserves model-architecture confidentiality through a private training specification. The protocol produces three proof types: a genesis proof at initialisation, in-training step proofs across the run, and ex-ante attestations enforcing policy-relevant claims as running invariants, turning the training record into a governance-enforceable artefact. We estimate a deployable proof of concept within approximately 36 months at single-digit-percent training-side overhead, against a six-to-ten-year cycle for verification-grade custom silicon. Thirteen open research and engineering problems are catalogued as a research agenda for external contribution

</details>


### [154] [Ten Headache Specialists versus Artificial Intelligence for Clinical Literature Summarization: A Critical Evaluation and Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05436)
*Alejandro Lozano,Keiko Ihara,Ping-Hao Yang,Carrie E. Robertson,Jennifer Stern,Allan Purdy,Hsiangkuo Yuan,Pengfei Zhang,Yulia Orlova,Olga Fermo,Jennifer Hranilovich,Fred Cohen,Todd J. Schwedt,Jenelle A. Jindal,Serena Yeung-Levy,Chia-Chun Chiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究构建了基于RAG的智能AI框架，使用三种大语言模型生成医学文献摘要，并与头痛专家撰写的摘要进行盲法比较。专家撰写的摘要更受欢迎，但专家有时难以区分人类与AI生成的摘要，同时识别出了超越标准评估指标的专家重视特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床医生面临时间有限和文献量激增的挑战，需要自动化工具辅助医学文献总结以支持循证医学决策。尽管RAG增强的LLM在临床总结中展现潜力，但缺乏人类专家对其合成更广泛科学文献效果的系统评估以及直接与专家撰写摘要的对比。

Method: 构建基于RAG的智能体AI框架，使用Sonnet、GPT-4o和Llama 3.1三种LLM。头痛专家设计13个问题（3个用于提示优化，10个用于评估）。10位头痛专家各撰写一份摘要，最终每个问题获得四份摘要（专家、Sonnet、GPT-4o、Llama）。专家在盲法条件下基于正确性、完整性、简洁性和临床效用进行1-10分评分，并进行偏好排序和作者身份判断。

Result: 专家撰写的摘要总体更受欢迎；但专家有时难以区分人类与AI生成的摘要；研究识别出了超越标准评估指标的专家重视特征，可用于指导未来文献摘要流程的改进。

Conclusion: 专家撰写的文献摘要优于LLM生成摘要，但两者的差距并非绝对明显；未来可结合专家重视的特征进一步优化人类和AI的文献摘要生成流程。

Abstract: Summarizing the latest medical literature to guide clinical decision-making is essential for evidence-based medicine and high-quality patient care. Yet clinicians face increasing challenges due to limited time with patients and a rapidly growing volume of published articles. Although retrieval-augmented large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in clinical summarization, human evaluations of their effectiveness in synthesizing broader scientific literature and direct comparisons to expert-written syntheses remain scarce. We constructed a RAG-based agentic AI framework using three state-of-the-art LLMs: Sonnet, GPT-4o, and Llama 3.1. A headache specialist created 13 questions, three for prompt optimization and ten for evaluation. Ten headache specialists across the United States and Canada each wrote a summary for one question, yielding four summaries per question (expert, Sonnet, GPT-4o, and Llama). The experts, blinded to authorship, critically evaluated the summaries, excluding the topic for which they wrote a summary, based on correctness, completeness, conciseness, and clinical utility, scoring each from 1 to 10 using standardized rubrics. They also ranked the summaries by preference and indicated whether they believed each summary was written by an expert or an LLM. Our study, comparing LLM- and expert-written literature summaries evaluated by headache specialists, showed that expert-written summaries were preferred, although experts sometimes found it challenging to distinguish between human- and AI-generated summaries. We also identified key expert-valued features beyond standard evaluation metrics that can guide future refinement of both human and AI literature summarization pipelines.

</details>


### [155] [Brick-Composer: Using MLLMs for Assembly with Diverse Bricks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05445)
*Jiateng Liu,Bingxuan Li,Zhenhailong Wang,Rushi Wang,Kaiwen Hong,Cheng Qian,Jiayu Liu,Denghui Zhang,Katherine Driggs-Campbell,Manling Li,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了BC-Bench基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在砖块组装任务中的能力，并提出了Brick-Composer学习框架，通过人类设计灵感、世界反馈和合成经验三种信号显著提升了MLLMs的砖块选择精度和姿态估计能力，将步骤级组装成功率从不到1%提升至约15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MLLMs在细粒度视觉定位和空间推理方面能力不足，而砖块组装任务恰好需要这两种能力；现有基准缺乏对砖块组装这一结构化构建任务的系统评估，因此需要建立专门的基准和方法来推动该方向研究。

Method: 将砖块组装形式化为序列决策问题，包含砖块选择（目标砖块识别）和砖块姿态估计（放置位置和方向预测）两个子任务；提出Brick-Composer框架，利用三种互补信号：Human Design Sparks（人类设计示范）、World Feedback（视觉和物理反馈）、Synthetic Experience（合成数据扩展学习）。

Result: Brick-Composer将砖块选择准确率提升3倍以上，显著降低姿态估计误差，将严格步骤级组装成功率从不到1%提升至约15%；基于Qwen-3-8B的模型可正确完成完整物体的42%步骤。

Conclusion: 当前MLLMs距离可靠的组装能力仍有较大差距，但通过针对性的物理接地学习，MLLMs能够习得组装能力，为未来实现从任意设计到真实物体构建的AI智能体迈出了第一步。

Abstract: We dream of AI agents that can read arbitrary designs and construct real-world objects from reusable building blocks. As a first step toward this vision, we study whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs) possess the visual grounding and spatial reasoning capabilities required for brick assembly. We formulate brick assembly as a sequential decision-making problem, where each step involves two subtasks: brick selection, identifying the target brick from candidate components, and brick pose estimation, predicting where and how the selected brick should be placed. To support this study, we introduce BC-Bench (Brick Construction Benchmark), the first benchmark for evaluating MLLMs on assembly with diverse bricks. Experiments show that current state-of-the-art MLLMs remain far from reliable builders, struggling with fine-grained brick selection and failing at precise pose estimation. To bridge this gap, we propose Brick-Composer, a learning framework that equips MLLMs with assembly skills through three complementary signals: Human Design Sparks, which provide affordance-rich construction demonstrations; World Feedback, which grounds predicted actions in visual and physical consequences; and Synthetic Experience, which scales learning beyond existing object designs. Brick-Composer improves brick selection accuracy by over three times, substantially reduces pose estimation errors, and raises strict step-level assembly success from less than 1% to around 15%. After training, a Qwen-3-8B can correctly compose up to 42% of the steps for a complete object, suggesting that MLLMs can acquire assembly capabilities through targeted, physically grounded learning.

</details>


### [156] [Insurance of Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05449)
*Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了代理型人工智能（Agentic AI）系统对保险市场的影响，分析了其带来的新型风险暴露（如幻觉、提示注入攻击、自主决策错误、模型漂移、依赖故障及网络物理危害），评估了现有保险产品（网络保险、专业责任、产品责任、董责险等）的适应性，提出了基于暴露评估、情景分析、依赖映射和累积风险管理的精算框架，并构建了一个整合网络保险、技术错误与遗漏、产品责任、履约保证及主动AI责任保险的分层保险生态体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理型AI系统具备自主规划、工具调用、决策执行及持久修改数字/物理环境的能能，所引入的新型风险暴露无法被传统保险类别（如网络保险、专业责任险、产品责任险、董责险）充分覆盖，保险市场亟需针对性的承保、定价、再保险及产品设计框架。

Method: 将代理型AI表征为自主性与授权程度的连续谱系，区分信息输出与能独立引发保险事件的系统；分析主要风险路径（幻觉、提示注入攻击、自主决策错误、模型漂移、依赖故障、网络物理危害）；评估现有保险产品的适应性；提出基于暴露评估、情景分析、依赖映射和累积风险管理的精算框架；构建整合多类覆盖的协调保险架构。

Result: 现有保险产品正在适应代理型AI风险暴露，但存在覆盖缺口；精算框架可借鉴网络保险演化经验；未来代理型AI保险并非单一险种，而是由互补覆盖组成的分层生态系统，需要明确的分配机制和专属AI聚合限额。

Conclusion: 代理型AI保险的未来在于构建一个由网络保险、技术错误与遗漏、产品责任、履约保证和主动AI责任保险等多层覆盖组成的协调生态体系，并依赖于治理改善、透明度提升、遥测技术和监管清晰度的支持。

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence (AI) systems are transforming the risk landscape by extending beyond information generation to autonomous planning, tool invocation, decision execution, and persistent modification of digital and physical environments. These capabilities introduce novel exposures that do not fit neatly within traditional insurance categories such as cyber, professional liability, product liability, or directors and officers coverage. This paper examines the emerging insurance market for agentic AI and develops a framework for understanding its underwriting, pricing, reinsurance, and product-design implications. We characterize agentic AI as a continuum of autonomy and delegated authority, emphasizing the distinction between informational outputs and systems capable of independently generating insured events through external actions. We analyze major risk pathways, including hallucinations, prompt-injection attacks, autonomous decision errors, model drift, dependency failures, and cyber-physical harms, and evaluate how existing insurance products are adapting to address these exposures. The paper further proposes an actuarial framework based on exposure assessment, scenario analysis, dependency mapping, and accumulation-risk management, drawing parallels to the evolution of cyber insurance. Finally, we present a coordinated insurance architecture that integrates cyber, technology errors and omissions, product liability, performance-warranty, and affirmative AI-liability coverages through explicit allocation mechanisms and dedicated AI aggregates. The analysis suggests that the future of agentic-AI insurance lies not in a single monoline product but in a layered ecosystem of complementary coverages supported by improved governance, transparency, telemetry, and regulatory clarity.

</details>


### [157] [Output Type Before Quality: A Standards-Derived XAI Admissibility Rubric for Autonomous-Driving Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05461)
*Abhinaw Priyadershi,Mandar Pitale,Jelena Frtunikj,Maria Spence*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有XAI方法（如SHAP）的输出类型与ADS安全标准所需的证据类型（有向因果链、量化干预效应等）之间存在“证据类型鸿沟”。作者从三大安全标准中提取19项可测试证据标准，评估六类XAI方法，发现因果XAI在危害识别、事故调查和数据管理三个阶段结构性地满足要求，而其他阶段相关或语言方法足够。该发现通过1,996个真实驾驶片段验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶安全标准要求特定形式的证据（有向因果链、量化干预效应、命名根因变量），但现有XAI文献按输出类型和技术家族组织，两者不匹配。最推荐的SHAP方法输出排序特征列表，无法转化为有向因果链，存在证据类型鸿沟。

Method: 从AMLAS、ISO 26262、ISO 21448、ISO/PAS 8800四个标准提取19项可测试证据标准，涵盖7个生命周期阶段，并给出代表性条款引证推导。然后对六类XAI方法进行结构性评分，并通过1,996个真实驾驶片段（79,840行，十折交叉验证）进行单-VLA概念验证。

Result: 因果XAI在三个生命周期阶段（危害识别+62%、事故调查+50%、数据管理+50%）结构性地优于其他方法；该结论在阈值T∈(0%,50%]内稳定，最坏情况单格翻转仍可维持至T=25%。其余四个阶段相关或语言方法可比或充分。概念验证结果与各方法输出类型匹配评分配对一致。

Conclusion: ADS安全保证的XAI方法选择应由生命周期阶段的证据需求驱动，而非方法流行度。结构可采纳性（评分通过）是必要条件而非充分条件，验证保真度（如SCM边、迹命名原因的正确性）是开放挑战。

Abstract: Safety standards for ML-based autonomous driving specify the kind of evidence an assurance case must contain (directed cause-and-effect chains, quantified interventional effects, named root-cause variables), yet the XAI literature is organised by output type and technique family (saliency maps, feature attribution, counterfactuals, causal graphs, language traces). SHAP, the most-recommended ADS XAI method, returns a ranked feature list that no implementation effort can convert into a directed chain (Fig.1). We name this mismatch the evidence-type gap.
  From AMLAS, ISO 26262, ISO21448, ISO/PAS 8800 we derive 19 testable evidentiary criteria across 7 lifecycle stages with representative clause-cited derivations and score six XAI method classes structurally.
  Causal XAI emerges as structurally required to satisfy the derived criteria at three stages: hazard identification (+62% rubric gap), incident investigation (+50%), and data management (+50%); the verdict set is stable across thresholds T in (0%, 50%]$ and survives a worst-case single-cell flip down to T = 25%. At the remaining four stages, correlational or language-based methods are comparable or sufficient. The rubric identifies structural admissibility (necessary but not sufficient for compliance): an admissible method's specific output content may still be wrong, and validating that fidelity (the edges a fitted SCM produces, the cause a trace names) is the open assurance challenge. A single-VLA proof of concept on 1,996 real-world driving clips (79,840 rows, ten splits) is consistent with each method's observed output type matching its rubric prediction. XAI method selection for ADS safety assurance should be driven by lifecycle-stage evidence demand, not by method popularity.

</details>


### [158] [PSEBench: A Controllable and Verifiable Benchmark for Evaluating LLMs in Patient Safety Event Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05463)
*Keqi Han,Ryan Young,Annabel Strauss,Lindsey Hughes,Katharine M. Nesbitt,Nicole Schueler,Che Ngufor,Carl Yang,Yuan Xue,Zhijun Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于策略的构建方法，利用条款卡（clause card）结构表示监管文本，生成PSEBench基准（5,074个案例），用于评估LLM在患者安全事件分类中的表现，揭示了现有模型的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者安全事件分类依赖人工专家完成，LLM可能辅助此任务，但缺乏能够捕捉基于证据的策略推理、主动信息寻求以及在模糊情况下合理弃权的基准来可靠评估LLM性能。

Method: 提出基于条款卡（clause card）的策略约束构建方法，将监管文本分解为可审计的决策规范；结合锚点驱动实例化与闭环验证的流水线，生成具有天然真实标签的叙事，并支持生成缺失信息和不确定变体；在明尼苏达州29种可报告不良健康事件上实例化，构建PSEBench。

Result: 构建了包含5,074个案例的PSEBench基准，并在15个代表性LLM上进行了评估，揭示了一致的能力趋势和可操作的差距。

Conclusion: PSEBench能够有效评估LLM在患者安全事件分类中的表现，识别出当前模型在策略推理、信息寻求和不确定情况下的弃权方面存在明显差距，为可靠部署LLM辅助该任务指明了方向。

Abstract: Patient safety event triage, determining whether a clinical event is reportable under jurisdiction-specific policy, is a high-stakes task typically performed manually by patient safety experts. Although LLMs may support this workflow, reliable evaluation is limited by the lack of benchmarks to capture evidence-grounded policy reasoning, proactive information seeking for incomplete reports, and principled abstention in irreducibly ambiguous cases. We address this gap with a policy-grounded construction methodology centered on the clause card, a structured representation that factorizes regulatory text into auditable decision specifications. Combining clause cards with anchor-driven instantiation and closed-loop verification, our scalable pipeline produces narratives with by-construction ground truth and naturally supports generating missing information and uncertain variants. We instantiate this method on Minnesota's 29 Reportable Adverse Health Events, producing PSEBench, a 5,074-case benchmark with an agentic evaluation environment. Evaluation on 15 representative LLMs reveals consistent capability trends, demonstrates the benchmark's utility, and identifies actionable gaps toward reliable LLM-based patient safety event triage.

</details>


### [159] [Step-by-Step Optimization-like Reasoning in LLMs over Expanding Search Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05464)
*Nicolás Astorga,Nabeel Seedat,Mihaela van der Schaar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OPT*是一个可扩展的优化式推理任务家族，通过可行性检查器和评估器定义任务，沿复杂度轴扩展搜索空间。论文提出了两种训练范式：求解器引导的在线策略优化（利用求解器作为状态价值函数，结合排名奖励塑造）和基于搜索的离线强化学习（无求解器时）。理论上将大搜索空间中的成功与每单位搜索预算提取的信息量相关联。实验表明该训练能提升LLM的逐步优化推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可验证奖励训练主要集中在数学和代码推理领域，但这些仅覆盖了逐步决策的一部分。现实世界任务需要在众多可行方案中找到高价值的可行计划，现有基准难以捕捉这种优化式推理的复杂性。

Method: 提出OPT*任务家族，每个任务包含可行性检查器和评估器，通过复杂度参数控制搜索空间规模。两种训练范式：(i) 求解器引导的在线策略优化——将求解器作为部分状态的价值预言机，使用基于排名的奖励塑造来强化更好的下一步；(ii) 基于搜索的离线强化学习——无求解器时使用搜索策略。

Result: 从理论上建立了大搜索空间中成功推理与每单位搜索预算提取信息量之间的关系。实验上消融了使搜索高效的各个要素，证明在OPT*上训练能提升LLM的逐步优化式推理能力。

Conclusion: OPT*为训练和评估LLM在优化类任务上的逐步推理能力提供了可扩展的框架，弥补了现有数学/代码推理基准之外的空白，两种训练范式分别适用于有/无求解器的场景，对提升LLM的优化推理具有实际意义。

Abstract: Verifiable reward training has improved mathematical and coding reasoning, but these domains capture only part of step-by-step decision making. Many real-world tasks require finding a high-value feasible plan among many valid alternatives. We introduce OPT*, a scalable family of optimization-style tasks for training and evaluating LLM step-by-step optimization-like reasoning along a complexity axis: each task provides a feasibility checker and evaluator, while a complexity parameter expands the search space without requiring new human labels. This motivates studying these tasks in two regimes: (i) solver-guided online policy optimization, which uses a solver as a value oracle for partial states and applies rank-based reward shaping to reinforce better next steps, and (ii) search-based offline RL when such solvers are unavailable. Theoretically, we relate success in large search spaces to the information a reasoner extracts per unit of search budget. Empirically, we ablate the ingredients that make search efficient on OPT* and show that training on OPT* improves step-by-step optimization-like reasoning.

</details>


### [160] [Severity-Aware Curriculum Learning with Multi-Model Response Selection for Medical Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05510)
*Ahmed Alansary,Molham Mohamed,Ali Hamdi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种严重性感知的多模型框架，结合课程训练策略与相关性响应选择，用于医疗文本生成。该框架采用三阶段课程学习（轻、中、重症），使用五个独立训练的LLM生成候选响应并选择最佳输出。在MAQA数据集上，BERTScore达到86.71%（基线）和90.30%（微调后），优于基线模型和微调模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在医疗问答中难以根据不同病例严重程度提供一致且上下文适当的响应，缺乏适应渐进式复杂性的能力。

Method: 提出严重性感知多模型框架，包含三阶段课程学习策略（轻、中、重症依次训练），五个LLM独立训练，推理时生成候选响应并通过相关性选择机制选出最佳输出。

Result: 在MAQA数据集上，BERTScore达到86.71%（基线设置）和90.30%（微调后），优于基线模型和微调模型。

Conclusion: 课程学习与多模型响应选择相结合能有效提升医疗文本生成的质量和相关性。

Abstract: Telehealth systems have become increasingly important for delivering accessible and timely medical information. Existing large language models often struggle to provide consistent and contextually appropriate medical responses across varying levels of case severity. This limitation highlights the need for models that can effectively adapt to the progressive complexity in medical queries. To address this challenge, we introduce a severity-aware multi-model framework that integrates curriculum training strategy with relevance-based response selection. The proposed framework employs a three-stage curriculum learning strategy, where each model is trained sequentially on mild, moderate, and critical cases to progressively acquire domain knowledge. The approach utilizes five large language models, each independently trained under the same curriculum scheme. During inference, all models generate candidate responses, and the most appropriate response is selected as the final output. The framework is trained and evaluated on the MAQA dataset, which provides annotated medical question-answer pairs. Experimental results evaluated using BERTScore demonstrate that the proposed method achieves superior performance compared to both baseline and fine-tuned models, attaining 86.71% in the baseline setting and 90.30% after fine-tuning. These results highlight the effectiveness of combining curriculum learning with multi-model response selection in improving response quality and relevance in medical text generation.

</details>


### [161] [EpiEvolve: Self-Evolving Agents for Streaming Pandemic Forecasting under Regime Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05513)
*Yiming Lu,Sihang Zeng,Zhengxu Tang,Max Lau,Fei Liu,Wei Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EpiEvolve是一种自进化智能体，通过层次化情景记忆、延迟标签反思和策略规则提炼，在流式COVID-19住院趋势预测中显著优于静态基线和CDC集成模型，并将体制转变后的恢复滞后从5周缩短至2周。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的流行病LLM预测器通常作为静态监督模型训练和评估，而实际疫情预测是流式过程——标签在预测后才到达，且疾病体制随时间变化。这种不匹配导致模型在动态环境中性能不佳。

Method: 提出EpiEvolve自进化智能体：包装固定权重的LLM预测器，通过层次化情景记忆存储预测结果，反思延迟标签，检索与当前体制相关的案例，并将重复错误提炼为策略规则。遵循严格的时间顺序协议防止未来信息泄漏。

Result: 在流式数据集上，EpiEvolve平均准确率达0.629，相比静态基线的0.561和外部CDC集成的0.325；体制转变后恢复滞后从5周降至2周。

Conclusion: 反思机制、策略记忆和体制感知检索对性能提升均有贡献，验证了自进化框架在动态流式预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Epidemic LLM forecasters are usually trained and evaluated as static supervised models, whereas operational pandemic forecasting is a streaming process in which labels arrive after predictions and disease regimes shift over time. We study this mismatch in weekly COVID-19 hospitalization trend forecasting across five variant regimes. We introduce EpiEvolve, a self-evolving agent that wraps an LLM forecaster trained on the warm-start period and keeps its weights fixed during streaming. EpiEvolve adapts by storing forecast outcomes in a hierarchical episodic memory, reflecting on delayed labels, retrieving cases relevant to the current regime, and distilling recurring errors into strategic rules. The resulting context lets the forecaster reuse its own past predictions and outcomes in later weeks while following a chronological protocol that prevents future leakage. On the streaming dataset, EpiEvolve reaches $0.629$ average accuracy, compared with $0.561$ for the static backbone and $0.325$ for the external CDC ensemble, and reduces recovery lag after regime shifts from $5$ to $2$ weeks. Ablations show that reflection, strategic memory, and regime-aware retrieval each contribute to the gains.

</details>


### [162] [SciVisAgentSkills: Design and Evaluation of Agent Skills for Scientific Data Analysis and Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05525)
*Kuangshi Ai,Haichao Miao,Kaiyuan Tang,Shusen Liu,Chaoli Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SciVisAgentSkills，一组可复用的智能体技能，用于增强编码智能体在科学可视化任务中的工具专业知识，通过在ParaView、napari、VMD和TTK等科学工具上编码环境假设、工具使用模式和领域启发式知识。在108个专家设计的多步任务基准上评估显示，这些技能能提升任务平均得分，其token效率取决于智能体框架和工具设置，强调了结构化程序性知识对于实现可靠的长时间跨度科学可视化工作流的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用编码智能体虽然能力强，但缺乏科学可视化任务所需的特定工具专业知识，导致在科学数据分析和可视化场景中表现不佳。

Method: 提出SciVisAgentSkills，一组可复用的智能体技能，通过编码环境假设、工具使用模式和领域启发式知识来增强编码智能体；在SciVisAgentBench（108个专家设计的多步任务）上使用Codex和Claude Code进行评估。

Result: 智能体技能提高了各评估套件的平均任务得分；token效率收益取决于智能体框架和工具设置。

Conclusion: 结构化程序性知识对于实现可靠的长时间跨度科学可视化工作流至关重要；技能应与加载和应用它们的执行框架一同研究。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic visualization have enabled the translation of natural language into executable scientific visualization (SciVis) workflows. While general-purpose coding agents show strong capabilities, they often lack the tool-specific expertise required for SciVis tasks. In this work, we present SciVisAgentSkills, a collection of reusable agent skills that augment coding agents for scientific data analysis and visualization by encoding environment assumptions, tool usage patterns, and domain heuristics across scientific tools such as ParaView, napari, VMD, and TTK. We evaluate these skills on Codex and Claude Code using SciVisAgentBench, a benchmark of 108 expert-designed multi-step tasks. Results show that agent skills improve mean task scores across the evaluated suites, with token-efficiency benefits that depend on the agent harness and tool setting. These findings highlight the importance of structured procedural knowledge for enabling reliable, long-horizon SciVis workflows, while also showing that skills should be studied alongside the execution harness that loads and applies them. The skills are available at https://github.com/KuangshiAi/SciVisAgentSkills.

</details>


### [163] [When Should We Protect AI? A Precautionary Framework for Consciousness Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05528)
*Anna Mikeda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于证据的预防性框架，将意识科学发现映射为渐进式保护义务，通过五个福利相关维度、阈值与梯度混合机制及跨维度聚合方法，帮助组织在不确定性下对可能有意识的AI系统做出负责任决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架仅评估AI系统是否可能具有意识，但没有指导如何根据评估结果采取行动，存在决策空白。

Method: 构建了三组件预防性框架：(1) 五个福利相关维度（现象意识、情感效价、元认知意识、自我叙事、能动性）；(2) 阈值加梯度的混合机制（二元触发新义务类别+连续缩放保护权重）；(3) 两种互补的跨维度聚合方法（层级法和架构无关法）。通过Replika和OpenClaw案例进行可操作化演示。

Result: 通过案例展示了不同维度空间位置如何触发不同义务类别，并为构建接近意识相关阈值的开发者提供设计指导。框架适用于神经、符号和神经符号系统。

Conclusion: 该框架使意识科学对组织决策具有实际参考价值，帮助在不确定性下做出知情决策。

Abstract: Existing frameworks assess whether AI systems might be conscious but provide no guidance on what to do with that assessment. We address this gap with a precautionary framework that maps consciousness evidence to graduated protective obligations. The framework comprises three components: (1) five welfare-relevant dimensions--phenomenal consciousness, affective valence, metacognitive awareness, self-narrative, and agency--each grounded in established consciousness science and linked to distinct moral concerns; (2) a threshold-plus-gradation hybrid specifying both binary triggers for new obligation categories and continuous scaling of protective weight; and (3) two complementary approaches to cross-dimensional aggregation, one hierarchical (drawing on Bach and Sorensen's Machine Consciousness Hypothesis) and one architecture-agnostic.
